3. Using Crypto Borrowing Products
a. Submitting a Loan Request . You may submit a request to enter into a Loan via the Platforms (each, an “ Order ”). The types of Digital Assets and (where available) Fiat Currency that may be borrowed pursuant to a Loan are determined by us at our discretion, may be updated from time to time, and will be made available to you via the Platforms. When submitting an Order, you must specify and confirm the parameters applicable to your requested Loan, including:
i. the type and amount of Digital Asset or Fiat Currency you wish to borrow (being the principal amount of the Loan, or “ Borrowed Asset ”);
ii. the Interest (as defined in Section 9) or annual percentage rate of the Loan;
iii. the duration of the Loan, which may be fixed or open-ended (the “ Term ”); and
iv. any other parameters displayed via the Platforms from time to time, including collateral type, and applicable leverage or margin levels;
(together, the “ Parameters ”).
b. Our Discretion . Submission of an Order does not oblige us to enter into a Loan with you. We may, at our sole discretion, accept, reject, limit, or cancel any Order, including based on eligibility criteria, collateral sufficiency, risk limits, market conditions, or legal or regulatory considerations.
c. Formation of a Loan . A Loan is formed only when Kraken accepts your Order and makes the Borrowed Asset available to you by crediting it to your Borrowing Enabled Account. At that point, a binding bilateral loan agreement is formed between you and us on the terms set out in this Crypto Borrowing Annex and the applicable Parameters, including the applicable Fees.
d. No Advice; Self-Directed Use . Any information displayed on the Platforms in connection with Crypto Borrowing Products, including indicative rates, collateral ratios, or loan terms, is provided for informational purposes only and does not constitute investment advice, credit advice, or a recommendation to enter into any Loan. You are solely responsible for determining whether to submit an Order and for selecting the Parameters.
e. Account and Platform Requirements . To use Crypto Borrowing Products, you must maintain an active Account in good standing and comply at all times with this Crypto Borrowing Annex and the Terms and any operational or technical requirements applicable to the Platforms. Kraken may impose limits on usage, functionality, or availability of Crypto Borrowing Product in respect of your Borrowing Enabled Account at any time.
f. Borrowing Limits . Our ability to submit an Order and to select the Parameters applicable to a Loan is subject to limits and restrictions determined by Kraken from time to time (the “ Borrowing Limits ”). Without limitation, Kraken may impose maximum or minimum borrow amounts, maximum or minimum Term lengths, or other restrictions on the Parameters that may be selected or applied to an Order. Applicable Borrowing Limits will be displayed to you via the Platforms. Borrowing Limits and other Parameter restrictions may be based on factors including, without limitation, the type and value of Collateral Assets, the type and value of the Borrowed Asset, applicable leverage or margin ratios, market conditions, Kraken’s internal risk management criteria, and Kraken’s legal or regulatory obligations. Borrowing Limits are monitored on an ongoing basis and may be amended, reduced, suspended, or removed by Kraken at any time where Kraken reasonably considers it appropriate. Where an applicable Borrowing Limit is reached, exceeded, or otherwise not satisfied, Kraken may prevent you from placing an Order, refuse to accept an Order, restrict your ability to increase or decrease an existing Loan, or otherwise restrict or suspend your use of Crypto Borrowing Products.
4. Collateral Requirement
a. Obligation to Provide Collateral . As a condition to entering into and maintaining any Loan, you must post and maintain eligible Collateral Assets in your Borrowing Enabled Account in the form and amounts determined by Kraken from time to time (the “ Collateral Requirement ”). The Collateral Requirement represents the amount and type of Collateral Assets required to open and support a Loan, taking into account any applicable Borrowing Limits, and will be shown to you via the Platforms in connection with your Order.
b. Eligible Collateral Assets . Collateral Assets may include such Digital Assets or Fiat Currency designated by us as eligible collateral from time to time. We may add, remove, suspend, or apply asset-specific haircuts or limits to Collateral Assets at our sole discretion.
c. Valuation of Collateral . Collateral Assets will be valued on a continuous basis at their fair market value as determined by us, including by reference to one or more internal or external pricing sources, and subject to applicable discounts, haircuts, liquidity adjustments, and conversion costs. Kraken may apply different valuation methodologies or adjustments to different types of Collateral Assets.
d. Maintenance; Monitoring . After a Loan has been opened, you must ensure that the value of your Collateral Assets remains at or above the minimum collateral threshold specified by Kraken to support that Loan on an ongoing basis (the “ Maintenance Margin Requirement ”). The Maintenance Margin Requirement represents the minimum value of Collateral Assets that must be maintained at all times in order to avoid an Event of Default or liquidation. You are solely responsible for monitoring your Collateral Assets and margin level and for taking any action necessary to maintain compliance with the Maintenance Margin Requirement.
e. Margin Calls . If the value of your Collateral Assets falls below the Maintenance Margin Requirement, Kraken may (but is not obliged to) notify you that you are required to post additional Collateral Assets or otherwise reduce your exposure (a “Margin Call”). You acknowledge and agree that Kraken is under no obligation to issue a Margin Call, to provide you with any minimum notice period to satisfy a Margin Call, or otherwise to notify you before exercising its rights under this Crypto Borrowing Annex, including the right to liquidate or realize Collateral Assets. Margin Calls and liquidations may occur at any time, including outside normal business hours. This is because the value of Collateral Assets may fall sharply and rapidly relative to the value of the Borrowed Asset, including outside normal business hours and over very short periods of time, such that any delay in exercising our rights to issue a Margin Call or to liquidate Collateral Assets could result in the value of the Collateral Assets being insufficient to satisfy your Obligations.
f. Restrictions . You may request to transfer, withdraw, or otherwise deal with your Collateral Assets (including any assets constituting Secured Assets for the purposes of this Crypto Borrowing Annex). However, where doing so would, or is reasonably likely to, result in an Event of Default, a breach of the Collateral Requirement, a breach of the Maintenance Margin Requirement, or a breach of any applicable Borrowing Limits in respect of any Loan, you may be required to repay or terminate the relevant Loan (in whole or in part) before such transfer, withdrawal, or other disposition.
g. Collateral Requirement Framework . Kraken may modify the Collateral Requirement, Maintenance Margin Requirement, eligible Collateral Assets, valuation methodology, or applicable haircuts at any time, including with immediate effect, where Kraken reasonably considers such modification necessary due to market conditions, risk management considerations, or legal or regulatory requirements.
5. Security
a. No Third-Party Security Interests . You must not create or permit any security interest, lien, charge, or other encumbrance over any Collateral Assets provided in connection with any Crypto Borrowing Product without Kraken’s prior written consent. You represent and warrant that all such Collateral Assets are free and clear of all liens and encumbrances other than those arising hereunder, and that you have full power and authority to grant the Security Interest described in this Crypto Borrowing Annex.
b. Secured Assets . Any and all Collateral Assets provided by you in connection with any Loan (the “ Secured Assets ”) are held as security on a continuing basis and are subject to a general lien and right of set-off in favour of us for the due performance and discharge of all present and future obligations, liabilities, and amounts owing by you to Kraken in connection with Crypto Borrowing Products, whether actual or contingent, including but not limited to the repayment of any Borrowed Assets, and payment of any Fees, interest, or other charges applicable to any loan or its repayment, Liquidation, or termination, whether by you or by us (the “ Obligations ”).
c. Grant of Security Interest . As continuing security for the Obligations, you hereby pledge, assign and grant to Kraken a first priority security interest over all of your right, title, and interest in and to the Secured Assets (the “ Security Interest ”). The Security Interest attaches upon the provision of the relevant Loan and continues until all Obligations have been irrevocably satisfied in full. You acknowledge and agree that, where an affiliate of ours holds, custodies, or otherwise controls any Secured Assets on your behalf, that entity is entitled to exercise any of our rights set out in this Section 5 to enforce the Security Interest or to give effect to any of our other rights under, and in accordance with, this Crypto Borrowing Annex and the Terms.
d. Nature of Security . The Security Interest is a continuing security and is not affected by any intermediate payment, settlement, compromise, or release of any Obligation, nor by any amendment, variation, extension, or renewal of any Loan.
e. Maintenance and Perfection . You must take all actions reasonably required by us to maintain the validity, enforceability, perfection, and priority of the Security Interest and to enable us to exercise or protect its rights in respect of the Secured Assets, including, but not limited to, (i) promptly discharging all liens on the Secured Assets other than our security interest or any other liens permitted herein and (ii) executing and delivering financing statements, control agreements, instruments of pledge, notices and assignments, in each case, in form and substance reasonably satisfactory to us, relating to the creation, validity, perfection, maintenance or continuation of our security interest in and lien on the Secured Assets under the UCC or other applicable law. We may take any action we reasonably consider necessary to perfect, maintain, or enforce the Security Interest, including through control, segregation, or internal book-entry arrangements. You hereby authorize us to file against you one or more financing, continuation or amendment statements pursuant to the UCC or other applicable law in form and substance reasonably satisfactory to us, provided that the description of the collateral in any such statement is limited to only the Secured Assets.
f. Enforcement of Security Interest . Kraken may enforce the Security Interest without prior notice or demand if:
i. an Event of Default occurs;
ii. the value of the Secured Assets falls below the Maintenance Margin Requirement; or
iii. enforcement is otherwise permitted under this Crypto Borrowing Annex or the Terms.
g. Exercise of Rights . In enforcing the Security Interest, Kraken may, without limitation and without prior notice:
i. demand and receive all amounts due in respect of any Obligation;
ii. exercise any rights you would otherwise have in respect of the Secured Assets; and
iii. apply, set off, convert, appropriate, or transfer any Secured Assets in or towards satisfaction of the Obligations, in such order and manner as Kraken reasonably determines.
h. Authorization . You irrevocably authorize Kraken to sell, appropriate, realize, or otherwise dispose of any Secured Assets and to apply the proceeds in satisfaction of the Obligations, including costs and expenses incurred in connection with enforcement. Where Kraken exercises a right of appropriation, Kraken may determine the value of the Secured Assets in good faith and in a commercially reasonable manner.
i. Set-Off . Without limiting any other rights, Kraken may set off any Obligations against any balances, assets, or proceeds held by us for your benefit, whether related to Crypto Borrowing Products or otherwise, and may convert assets as reasonably necessary to effect such set-off.
6. Liquidation
a. Trigger . Kraken may liquidate, sell, convert, or otherwise realize any Secured Assets (a “ Liquidation ”) if:
i. the value of your Collateral Assets falls below the Maintenance Margin Requirement, or you attempt to take any action that would be reasonably likely to result in the value of your Collateral Assets falling below the Maintenance Margin Requirement;
ii. an Event of Default occurs; or
iii. a Loan reaches its Term and remains outstanding.
Such Liquidation may result in the partial or full termination of one or more Loans.
b. Notice . You acknowledge and agree that Kraken is under no obligation to provide you with prior notice of any Liquidation, Margin Call, or enforcement action. Liquidations may occur automatically, without notice, and at any time, including outside normal business hours.
c. Liquidation Process and Discretion. In carrying out a Liquidation, we may, at our sole discretion and without limitation:
i. determine the timing, method, order, and extent of any sale, conversion, or realization of Secured Assets;
ii. sell, convert, or otherwise dispose of Secured Assets at a price which we consider appropriate in the circumstances, including where market conditions are volatile, illiquid, or dislocated, and apply any proceeds of Liquidation in or towards satisfaction of the Obligations.
You acknowledge and agree that we have no obligation to obtain any particular price, minimize losses, or optimize outcomes for you when carrying out a Liquidation. Market conditions, liquidity constraints, and execution timing may result in Liquidation at adverse prices. You will be responsible for any shortfall in your account after such Liquidation.
7. Default
a. Default; Consequences . Where the value of your Collateral Assets falls below the applicable Maintenance Margin Requirement for one or more Loans, or you otherwise are, or in our reasonable determination are likely to be, unable to meet your Obligations in respect of one or more Loans, including your obligations to repay the Loan, maintain required Collateral Assets, or pay Fees, interest, or other charges when due (an “ Event of Default ”), Kraken may, without prior notice and without prejudice to any other rights or remedies:
i. declare any or all Obligations immediately due and payable;
ii. suspend, restrict, or terminate your access to any Crypto Borrowing Product;
iii. enforce the Security Interest in accordance with Section 5;
iv. liquidate or realize Secured Assets in accordance with Section 6; and/or
v. exercise any rights of set-off or netting available under this Crypto Borrowing Annex or the Terms.
b. No Waiver . Kraken’s failure or delay in exercising any right or remedy upon an Event of Default does not constitute a waiver of that Event of Default or of any subsequent Event of Default.
c. Payment to Kraken . If the value of your Collateral Assets ever becomes less than the value of your Borrowed Assets (i.e., "negative equity"), as determined by us, whether as a result of liquidation, market conditions, or otherwise, you agree to pay the full amount of funds owed to Kraken within 48 hours of demand. You may not trade or withdraw on a negative equity account. Your obligation to repay any negative balance or deficiency shall survive the termination of any Loan and this Crypto Borrowing Annex.
8. Repayment, Termination, and Maturity
a. Term and Maturity . Each Loan is entered into for the Term specified in the applicable Order. Unless terminated earlier in accordance with this Crypto Borrowing Annex, a Loan will mature automatically at the end of its Term (the “ Maturity Date ”). Where the Term selected by you is open-ended and not fixed, the Loan shall have no Maturity Date and shall continue until closed by you or terminated by us in accordance with this Crypto Borrowing Annex.
b. Repayment at Maturity . On the Maturity Date, you must repay the Loan together with any accrued but unpaid Fees, interest, and other charges applicable to the Loan.
c. Early Repayment . You may, subject to any restrictions shown on the Platforms, repay a Loan in whole or in part prior to the Maturity Date. Any early repayment shall not relieve you of the obligation to pay any Fees (including Early Repayment Fees) or interest that have accrued or are otherwise payable in accordance with this Crypto Borrowing Annex. Where the Term of a Loan is open-ended and not fixed, you may close the Loan at any time, whether before or after notice of a change to the Interest rate under Section 9, without incurring an Early Repayment Fee.
d. Method of Repayment . Repayment of the Loan is required to be made in the same type of Digital Asset or Fiat Currency as the Borrowed Asset. Where you do not hold sufficient balances of the Borrowed Asset in your Borrowing Enabled Account to satisfy any Obligations when due, you irrevocably authorize Kraken to apply, convert, or realize other Collateral Assets in order to satisfy such obligations, which may incur conversion fees as detailed in Section 9 of this Crypto Borrowing Annex.
e. Termination by Us . Without prejudice to Kraken’s rights under Sections 5 (Security), 6 (Liquidation), and 7 (Default), we may terminate and accelerate repayment of a Loan, in whole or in part, prior to the Maturity Date (where specified), where:
i. such repayment or termination is required in order for Kraken or any Kraken Affiliate to comply with applicable law, regulation, sanctions, or regulatory guidance;
ii. we have notified you of a material change to, or discontinuation of, a Crypto Borrowing Product, either generally or in relation to you specifically, which requires you to repay, terminate, or otherwise close one or more outstanding Loans, and you have failed to do so within the applicable notice period (if any); or
iii. we have made reasonable but unsuccessful attempts to contact you in connection with your Loan, your Account has been inactive for a period that we consider significant in the circumstances, and we have made a reasonable determination that allowing the Loan to continue would materially and adversely affect the value of your Collateral Assets.
f. Effect of Termination or Repayment . Upon the termination or repayment in full of a Loan:
i. all outstanding Obligations in respect of that Loan become immediately due and payable;
ii. Kraken may exercise its rights under Sections 5 (Security) and 6 (Liquidation) to enforce the Security Interest and liquidate Secured Assets as necessary.
g. No Obligation to Renew or Extend . We have no obligation to renew, roll over, or extend any Loan beyond its Term.
h. Suspension of Liquidation, Interest . We may suspend any Loan, including the Liquidation or charging of Interest in respect of a Loan, in order for us or any of our Affiliates to comply with applicable law, regulation, sanctions, or regulatory guidance.
9. Fees, Interest, and Charges
a. General . We may impose fees, interest, or other charges in connection with any Crypto Borrowing Product and any Loan, including Interest, Origination Fees, Early Repayment Fees, Liquidation Fees, and Currency Conversion Fees (together, “ Fees ”). Fees shall be imposed or charged at the applicable rates and methods of computation specified in the Fee Schedule, and form part of, and be binding terms of, the relevant Loan once your Order is accepted. Certain Fees may be determined by reference to the Parameters selected by you at the time the Order is placed. Payment of any Fees by you forms part of the Obligations and may be debited from your Borrowing Enabled Account.
b. Interest . Each Loan bears interest at the rate displayed to you via the Platform at the time your Loan Request is submitted, which accrues on the outstanding principal amount of the Loan, and is charged and payable by you periodically every four (4) hours during the Term (“ Interest ”). Once your Order is accepted by us, such Interest rate shall form part of and be binding terms of the relevant Loan. We may display to you via the platform the annualised Interest rate for convenience, however the binding Interest rate shall be that rate which accrues, and is charged and payable by you periodically every four (4) hours during the Term. Interest is payable in-kind, in the same type of Digital Asset as the Borrowed Asset.
c. Interest Changes . Where the Term of a Loan is open-ended and not fixed, Kraken may change the applicable Interest rate by giving you not less than thirty (30) days’ prior written notice of the modified rate and the date and time from which it shall take effect (a “ Rate Change Notice ”). Upon expiry of the notice period specified in the Rate Change Notice, the modified Interest rate shall become binding on you and your Loan shall, from the date and time specified in the Rate Change Notice, accrue Interest at the modified rate. The effect of the foregoing is to vary the existing terms of your Loan, such that the modified rate forms part of and becomes a binding term of the Loan.
We may modify the Interest rate for commercial reasons, including: (a) changes to our cost of capital, or to the cost to us of obtaining, holding, or maintaining the assets we lend to you, including changes in market reference rates or wholesale funding costs in the relevant Digital Asset (for example, where it costs us more to lend you assets, that may result in an increase to the Interest rate, and vice versa); and (b) changes in demand for loans (for example, where there is higher demand for assets, that may result in an increase to the Interest rate, and vice versa); and (c) changes in the availability of the Borrowed Asset to us (for example, where the Borrowed Asset becomes scarcer or less liquid in the market, that may result in an increase to the Interest rate, and vice versa); or (d) other reasons not listed here, provided that this is a reasonable and proportionate response to the relevant event that has prompted the Interest rate change.
We shall not issue Rate Change Notices increasing the Interest rate applicable to Loans denominated in a given Borrowed Asset more frequently than once in any thirty (30) day period, measured from the effective date of the most recent Rate Change Notice that increased the Interest rate applicable to Loans denominated in that Borrowed Asset. For the avoidance of doubt, this restriction does not apply to Rate Change Notices that decrease the Interest rate, which may be issued at any time.
Where, after we have issued a Rate Change Notice in respect of Loans denominated in a Borrowed Asset but before the modified Interest rate has taken effect, you (i) enter into a new Loan with an open-ended Term in respect of that Borrowed Asset, or (ii) increase the principal amount of an existing Loan with an open-ended Term in respect of that Borrowed Asset, then provided that information about the modified Interest rate and the date and time from which it shall take effect is displayed to you via the Platforms at the time you submit the relevant Order or request to increase the Loan: (a) the Rate Change Notice shall be deemed to have been given to you at that time in respect of, as applicable, the new Loan or the increased principal amount of the existing Loan; and (b) the modified Interest rate shall, on the date and time specified in the Rate Change Notice, form part of and become a binding term of, as applicable, the new Loan or the increased Loan.
d. Origination . We may charge a one-time origination fee in connection with the opening of a Loan (“ Origination Fee ”). Any Origination Fee will be disclosed to you via the Platforms prior to submission of your Order.
e. Early Repayment . Where you repay a Loan prior to its Maturity Date, Kraken may charge an early repayment fee as displayed to you via the Platform and/or Site from time to time (“Early Repayment Fee”). Any such Early Repayment Fee is payable in-kind, in the same type of Digital Asset as the Borrowed Asset. For the avoidance of doubt, no Early Repayment Fee shall be payable in respect of the closure or repayment of a Loan with an open-ended Term.
f. Liquidation . We may charge a fee in connection with the realization of Secured Assets in connection with any Liquidation (“ Liquidation Fee ”). Liquidation Fees are payable in-kind, in the same Digital Asset as the Borrowed Asset, form part of the Obligations, and may be deducted from the proceeds of any Liquidation or otherwise debited from your Borrowing Enabled Account.
g. Currency Conversion . Where you do not hold sufficient balances of the Borrowed Asset in your Borrowing Enabled Account to satisfy any Obligations when due, you authorize us to convert other assets in your Borrowing Enabled Account, including Collateral Assets, into the Borrowed Asset. In circumstances where multiple asset types are available for conversion, Kraken may apply a conversion priority framework determined by us and disclosed to you via the Platforms, as amended from time to time. This framework may give preference to the conversion of certain asset types over others (for example, converting available Fiat Currency before converting Digital Assets). Where we convert other assets to satisfy Obligations, such conversion shall not constitute a discharge of those obligations unless and until we have applied the relevant amounts in accordance with this Crypto Borrowing Annex. Conversions may occur at prices and rates determined by Kraken in good faith and in a commercially reasonable manner. You acknowledge and agree that Kraken has no obligation to obtain any particular price, minimize losses, or optimize outcomes for you in connection with any such conversion, and that market conditions, liquidity constraints, and execution timing may result in adverse pricing. We may charge you a fee in connection with such conversions (“ Currency Conversion Fee ”), which may be deducted from the proceeds of any sale of Collateral Assets or otherwise debited from your Borrowing Enabled Account.
h. Tax treatment . For US tax purposes, in the case of a Loan, it is intended that, absent a change in law or administrative practice to the contrary, the transfer and delivery of the Digital Assets or Fiat Currency shall be treated as a loan and not be treated as an exchange of property for other property differing materially in kind or extent (within the meaning of Section 1001 of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986, as amended, as well as the corresponding Treasury Regulations). You are solely responsible for determining the tax treatment and paying any taxes arising from your use of Crypto Borrowing Products. You remain responsible for any taxes required by applicable law in connection with payments you make under a Loan. Any Fees (including Interest) imposed by us are determined net of all taxes.
10. Risks
a. General Risk Acknowledgement . You acknowledge and agree that using Crypto Borrowing Products and entering into Loans involves significant risk and may result in substantial or total loss of your Collateral Assets. Crypto Borrowing Products are suitable only for users who understand and are able to bear these risks.
b. Market Risk . Digital Asset markets are highly volatile and prices may fluctuate rapidly and unpredictably. Movements in the value of the Borrowed Asset or Collateral Assets may occur over very short periods of time and may materially and adversely affect your position. Past performance or historical data does not constitute a reliable indicator of future performance.
c. Liquidation Risk . If the value of your Collateral Assets falls below the applicable Maintenance Margin Requirement, or if Kraken otherwise exercises its rights under this Crypto Borrowing Annex, your Collateral Assets may be liquidated without prior notice. Liquidation may occur automatically, at any time, including outside normal business hours, and may result in the loss of some or all of your Collateral Assets.
d. Variable Interest Rate Risk. Where the Term of your Loan is open-ended, the Interest rate applicable to your Loan is variable and may be increased by Kraken on not less than thirty (30) days’ prior notice in accordance with Section 9. Increases to the Interest rate may materially increase the cost to you of the Loan over time. If you do not wish to accept a modified Interest rate, your remedy is to close your Loan in accordance with Section 8, which will require you to repay the Borrowed Asset together with any accrued but unpaid Interest, Fees, and other charges. Closing your Loan may require you to convert or realise Collateral Assets, which may result in losses, including as a result of adverse market conditions, illiquidity, volatility, or conversion fees.
e. No Obligation to Optimize Outcomes . You acknowledge and agree that Kraken has no obligation to obtain any particular price, minimize losses, or optimize outcomes for you when carrying out any liquidation, conversion, or enforcement action. Market conditions, liquidity constraints, and execution timing may result in adverse pricing.
f. Conversion and Liquidity Risk . Where assets are converted in order to satisfy any Obligations, such conversions may occur at prices determined by Kraken in good faith and in a commercially reasonable manner. Due to market conditions, illiquidity, or volatility, conversions may result in losses or adverse pricing outcomes.
g. Technology, Protocol, and Operational Risks . Digital Assets and related networks are subject to technological risks, including protocol changes, forks, airdrops, network congestion, smart-contract vulnerabilities (where applicable), cyber-attacks, and operational failures. Such events may affect the value, transferability, or availability of the Borrowed Asset or Collateral Assets and may impair Kraken’s ability to perform actions under this Crypto Borrowing Annex.
h. Regulatory and Legal Risk . The regulatory treatment of Digital Asset and Fiat Currency lending, Digital Assets, and related activities is evolving and uncertain. Changes in law, regulation, or regulatory interpretation may adversely affect the Crypto Borrowing Products, your ability to use the service, or the value or treatment of Loans or Collateral Assets, including by requiring modification, suspension, or termination of the service.
i. No Advice . Kraken does not provide investment, legal, tax, or accounting advice in connection with any Crypto Borrowing Product. You are solely responsible for determining whether entering into a Loan is appropriate for you, including evaluating your financial situation, objectives, and risk tolerance.
j. Correlation Risk . You acknowledge that the value of the Borrowed Asset and the Collateral Assets may be correlated and may decline simultaneously, which may increase the likelihood of liquidation and result in rapid loss of Collateral Assets.

Kraken does not provide money transmission or foreign exchange services.The Kraken interface is solely intended to (a) collect and relay your instructions and relevant information to MoneyGram; (b) display MoneyGram-provided disclosures (including FX Quotes and fees); and (c) securely relay request metadata to MoneyGram. Annex Q: Kraken Prop Terms
1. What is Kraken Prop and Kraken Funded?

The evaluation service is provided by Breakout Trading Group, LLC ("Breakout") and the funded trading arrangement, if you pass the evaluation, is operated by Payward Oceanic Ltd., a company incorporated in the British Virgin Islands ("Payward Oceanic"). Kraken is not a party to either arrangement.
2. Third-Party Agreements
a. Before purchasing an evaluation, you must accept Breakout's Evaluation Terms of Service, which govern the evaluation process, fees, and outcomes. If you pass, you will be required to sign a Funded Trader Agreement ("FTA") directly with Payward Oceanic before any funded trading arrangement is activated.
b. Those agreements, not these terms, set out the full details of how the programme works, the risks involved, your profit entitlement, and the obligations of each party.
c. All trading signals submitted during the evaluation or funded stage are simulated and funds used are virtual. These signals are used for evaluation purposes or by Payward Oceanic to assess whether to act on a given signal.
3. Eligibility
a. To access Kraken Prop you must:
be at least 18 years of age;
hold a valid, verified Kraken Pro account in good standing; and
be located in a jurisdiction where Kraken Prop is made available (see Section 4).
4. Availability
a. Kraken Prop is available only in jurisdictions where Kraken determines it is permissible to offer access to the programme. Availability may be restricted or withdrawn at any time without prior notice, including following regulatory guidance. By accessing Kraken Prop you confirm you are not in a restricted jurisdiction.
5. Kraken's Role and Liability
a. Kraken provides the platform interface only. Kraken does not operate the evaluation, make execution decisions, or hold funds on your behalf in connection with Kraken Prop. Kraken is not responsible for the acts or omissions of Breakout or Payward Oceanic, for the outcome of any evaluation, or for any payment due to you under the FTA.
6. Suspension and Amendments
a. Kraken may suspend or remove your access to Kraken Prop at any time, including where you breach this Agreement or where Kraken decides to discontinue the feature. Kraken may amend these terms. Continued use following the effective date of any amendment constitutes acceptance of the revised terms.

RISK NOTICE
The Advice and Portfolio Management Service involves personalised investment recommendations and, at the AI Portfolio Management level, the autonomous execution of transactions on your account within parameters set by you. These services involve risk, including: the risk that recommendations may not be suited to your individual circumstances; the risk that automated systems generate inaccurate, incomplete, or anomalous outputs; and the risk that market conditions change materially after a recommendation is generated. You should not rely solely on outputs generated by the service and should apply your own judgement. Past recommendations are not indicative of future performance. The AI Portfolio Management service operates within parameters you set, but the automated system will execute transactions without your per-transaction approval; you should ensure your parameters and guardrails are appropriate to your circumstances before activating the service.
1. Advice and Portfolio Management Service
This Advice and Portfolio Management Annex governs your access to and use of the Advice and Portfolio Management Service. The Advice and Portfolio Management Service comprises the following tiers, which are made available to eligible users in eligible jurisdictions:
(a) AI Investment Advice. The provision of personalised investment recommendations to you in respect of transactions available on our Services and Platforms, generated having regard to your individual circumstances, risk appetite, objectives, and financial position. Each recommendation requires your explicit per-recommendation approval before it is executed on your account. AI Investment Advice is provided by the applicable Advisory Entity.
(b) AI Portfolio Management. The discretionary management of your portfolio, including the execution of investment decisions within parameters and guardrails set by you, without requiring your approval for each individual transaction. AI Portfolio Management is the highest tier of the Advice and Portfolio Management Service and involves the autonomous execution of transactions on your account. AI Portfolio Management is provided by the applicable Advisory Entity.
2. Use of Automated Systems
The Advice and Portfolio Management Service is delivered using automated analytical tools incorporating artificial intelligence and machine learning. By accessing the Advice and Portfolio Management Service, you acknowledge and accept that:
(c) System-generated outputs. Investment recommendations and portfolio management outputs are generated by automated systems, subject to ongoing system-level monitoring and periodic testing by our compliance and risk management functions;
(d) Output limitations. Automated systems may produce inaccurate, incomplete, or anomalous outputs, and you should exercise your own judgement before acting on any recommendation;
(e) AI Investment Advice — per-recommendation approval required. At the AI Investment Advice level, your explicit approval is required before any recommendation or series of recommended actions is executed on your account. You should apply your own judgement and, where appropriate, seek independent professional advice before acting;
(f) AI Portfolio Management — autonomous execution. At the AI Portfolio Management level, the system will execute transactions within your defined parameters without per-transaction approval; and
(g) Right to pause and stop. You retain the right to pause, modify, or stop the Advice and Portfolio Management Service at any time through the Platforms without contacting our support team.
3. AI Identity Disclosure
The Advice and Portfolio Management Service is an AI-powered service. You acknowledge that outputs presented through the Advice and Portfolio Management Service are generated by automated systems.
4. Consent Gate for AI Portfolio Management
Before the AI Portfolio Management service is activated on your account, we will present you with a clear explanation of: (i) the scope of transactions the system may execute on your account; (ii) the parameters and guardrails within which it will operate; and (iii) how to pause, modify, or stop the service. You must affirmatively consent to activation of the AI Portfolio Management service before it is enabled.
You may withdraw consent and deactivate AI Portfolio Management at any time through the Platforms without contacting our support team. Withdrawal of consent will not affect any transactions already executed pursuant to the service.
Where the Advice and Portfolio Management Service allows you to escalate from AI Investment Advice to AI Portfolio Management, a distinct consent gate will be presented at the point of transition. You must affirmatively consent to the transition before AI Portfolio Management is activated. By consenting to the transition, you are authorising the applicable Advisory Entity to execute transactions on your account without seeking your per-transaction approval, within the parameters and guardrails you have set. The consent gate for AI Portfolio Management is not satisfied by any consent you have given in connection with AI Investment Advice.
5. Parameters and Guardrails
At the AI Portfolio Management level, you will set the parameters and guardrails within which the automated system will operate. These may include the asset classes in scope, risk limits, maximum position sizes, and loss thresholds. The system will not execute transactions outside your defined parameters. You may modify your parameters at any time through the Platforms.
You remain the owner of all Digital Assets and other assets in your account at all times. The Advice and Portfolio Management Service does not transfer ownership of or title to your assets. Section 2 of the Terms (Custody and Title of Digital Assets) applies to all assets in your account, including those subject to the Advice and Portfolio Management Service.
6. Conflicts of Interest
We acknowledge that the Advice and Portfolio Management Service gives rise to an inherent structural conflict of interest: recommendations generated through the service may direct your activity toward products and services available on our Platforms, which generates transaction fee revenue for us. We manage this conflict through ongoing compliance monitoring of recommendation outputs and associated trading activity.
7. Fees
We do not charge a separate advisory fee or management fee for the Advice and Portfolio Management Service. Revenue is generated through transaction fees applied to trades executed pursuant to investment recommendations or portfolio management instructions, consistent with our existing fee model as set out in Section 6 of the Terms. We do not charge performance fees or fees calculated by reference to assets under management.
8. Service Monitoring and Material Changes
We maintain ongoing monitoring and periodic testing of the automated systems used to deliver the Advice and Portfolio Management Service. We will notify you of any material changes to the Advice and Portfolio Management Service through the Platforms where reasonably practicable. In the event of a material failure or anomaly in the automated systems, we may suspend the Advice and Portfolio Management Service or operate it in a reduced functionality mode, without liability to you for losses arising from either such suspension or reduction in functionality, until the issue is resolved.
9. Feedback and Contestability
You may raise concerns, errors, or complaints about outputs generated by the Advice and Portfolio Management Service through the support centre at https://support.kraken.com. All concerns will be assessed by the responsible product team in accordance with our complaints handling procedure as set out in the Terms.
10. Suspension and Termination
You may cease using the Advice and Portfolio Management Service at any time through the Platforms. Termination will stop the system from executing further transactions pursuant to the Advice and Portfolio Management Service but will not unwind any transactions already executed. All assets in your account remain yours.
We may suspend or terminate the Advice and Portfolio Management Service on reasonable notice, or immediately where required by applicable law or regulation, or where we determine in our sole discretion that continued provision of the service is not appropriate. Suspension or termination of the Advice and Portfolio Management Service will not affect any rights you have in respect of transactions already executed. Our rights to suspend or terminate your account more generally are set out in Section 15 of the Terms.
Annex S: Kraken Legacy
This Annex S (this " Kraken Legacy Annex ") incorporates by reference and supplements the Kraken.com Terms of Service (excluding all Annexes thereto, "Terms") as if the entirety of the Terms were set forth in this Kraken Legacy Annex. Capitalized terms used but not defined in this Kraken Legacy Annex have the meanings given to them elsewhere in the Terms. In the event of a conflict between the Terms and this Kraken Legacy Annex, the terms of this Kraken Legacy Annex will control solely to the extent of the conflict and solely with respect to the subject matter of this Kraken Legacy Annex.
1. Scope and Relationship to Main Terms
This Annex S governs your use of Kraken Legacy (“Kraken Legacy” or "Legacy"), a digital asset discovery and recovery service offered by Kraken. These terms supplement and form part of the Kraken Terms of Service. Legacy is only available in jurisdictions where Kraken has expressly made it available, and only to eligible users as set out in Section S.2.
2. Eligibility
To use Legacy you must: (a) have an existing Kraken account in good standing; and (b) have completed Kraken's standard know-your-customer identity verification process and be eligible to deposit and withdraw Digital Assets on the Kraken platform. Legacy is not available to users located in New York State. Kraken may impose additional eligibility requirements at any time and may withdraw access to Legacy at its sole discretion.
3. Description of Service
Legacy allows you to submit seed phrases, private keys, or hardware wallet backups (" Recovery Material ") to Kraken for the purpose of: (a) scanning supported blockchain networks to identify Digital Assets associated with your Recovery Material ("Discovery"); and (b) if you elect to proceed, transferring those Digital Assets into your Kraken custodial account ("Recovery"). The specific recovery methods available to you are set out in the sub-annex applicable to your jurisdiction.
Legacy is a technical facilitation service only. It is not a financial advisory, legal, tax, estate planning, or wealth management service. Kraken does not provide any legal advice in connection with Legacy, including advice on the ownership, valuation, tax treatment, or legal status of any Digital Assets discovered or recovered.
4. Temporary Holding of Recovery Material
When you submit Recovery Material, Kraken will temporarily hold that material in a secure, isolated environment solely for the purpose of conducting Discovery and, if elected, Recovery. Your Recovery Material is only held for the duration of the process and is permanently purged from Kraken's systems once the process is complete or if you choose to exit before the process is complete. Kraken does not retain, store, or have ongoing access to your Recovery Material beyond what is strictly necessary to complete the service. The temporary holding of Recovery Material under this Annex S is not a wallet custody service and does not constitute an exchange deposit relationship.
5. Your Representations and Warranties
By using Legacy, you represent and warrant to Kraken that: (a) you are the lawful owner of, or have lawful authority to access, transfer, and recover, all Digital Assets associated with the Recovery Material you submit; (b) you are not aware of any competing, adverse, or third-party claims to ownership of any Digital Assets associated with your Recovery Material; (c) you are not submitting Recovery Material on behalf of any third party who has not authorised you to do so; (d) to the best of your knowledge your use of Legacy complies with all applicable laws in your jurisdiction, including any laws governing the inheritance, transfer, or administration of Digital Assets or estates; and (e) you have not obtained the Recovery Material through theft, fraud, or any unlawful means.
Kraken does not verify, adjudicate, or determine ownership or legal entitlement to any Digital Assets discovered or recovered through Legacy. Responsibility for confirming lawful ownership rests entirely with you.
If you have received Recovery Material as part of a deceased person's estate, you should confirm your legal entitlement under applicable succession law before using Legacy. If you are in any doubt as to your legal entitlement, you should obtain independent legal advice before proceeding.
6. No Legal, Tax, or Estate Advice
Legacy does not constitute legal, tax, financial, or estate planning advice. Kraken is not acting as your legal adviser, tax adviser, financial adviser, or estate administrator in connection with Legacy. Discovery of Digital Assets does not establish legal ownership. Recovery of Digital Assets does not constitute a determination of lawful entitlement. You should seek independent legal and tax advice regarding any estate, inheritance, or ownership matters arising in connection with your use of Legacy.
Where you are accessing Legacy as a client of Payward Ltd. in the United Kingdom: Legacy has been designed to meet the FCA's Consumer Duty standards for retail clients, including the consumer understanding outcome under the FCA's Consumer Duty (PS22/9). If anything in the Legacy service or these terms is unclear to you, please contact Kraken's customer support before proceeding.
7. Discovery Report
Upon completion of Discovery, Kraken will provide you with a Discovery Report summarising the Digital Assets identified in connection with your Recovery Material. The Discovery Report is for informational purposes only. It does not guarantee the recoverability of any assets, constitute a valuation, or establish legal ownership. Kraken makes no representation that the Discovery Report is complete or accurate with respect to all blockchain networks, wallet formats, or asset types.
8. Irreversibility of Recovery
Recovery transactions, once initiated, are irreversible. Kraken cannot cancel, reverse, or recover any Digital Assets once a Recovery transaction has been confirmed on the applicable blockchain network. You should review the Discovery Report and asset selection carefully before confirming Recovery.
9. Fees
Any applicable fees will be clearly disclosed to you before you initiate Recovery, in your local currency or percentage terms, and will require your affirmative acknowledgement before any fee is charged.
10. AML, Sanctions, and Compliance
All Recovery transactions are subject to Kraken's standard AML, sanctions screening, and transaction monitoring controls. Recovered Digital Assets flow through the same deposit screening processes as standard Kraken deposits. Kraken may delay, block, or decline Recovery transactions where required by applicable law or Kraken's compliance programme.
If you are accessing Legacy as a client of Payward Ltd. in the United Kingdom: all Recovery transactions are additionally subject to Kraken's obligations under the Proceeds of Crime Act 2002 and the Money Laundering Regulations 2017. Kraken may delay, block, or decline Recovery transactions where assets are the subject of a production order, restraint order, or other court or law enforcement process.
11. Indemnification
In addition to the indemnification obligations set out in the main Terms of Service, you agree to indemnify, defend, and hold harmless Kraken and its affiliates, officers, directors, employees, and agents from and against any and all claims, losses, liabilities, damages, costs, and expenses (including reasonable legal fees) arising out of or relating to: (a) your submission of Recovery Material that you were not lawfully authorised to submit; (b) the recovery of Digital Assets to which you did not have lawful entitlement; (c) any third-party claim to ownership of Digital Assets discovered or recovered through your use of Legacy; or (d) your breach of any representation or warranty in Section S.5. The applicable offering entity for indemnification purposes is set out in the sub-annex for your jurisdiction.
12. Disclaimer of Warranties
Legacy is provided "as is" and "as available." Kraken makes no warranty, express or implied, that Legacy will identify all Digital Assets associated with your Recovery Material, that Discovery will be complete or error-free, or that Recovery will be successful for all identified assets. Asset recovery depends on blockchain network conditions, supported asset types, and other factors outside Kraken's control.
If you are accessing Legacy as a client of Payward Ltd. in the United Kingdom: nothing in this Section S.12 excludes, restricts, or modifies any right or remedy you are entitled to under the Consumer Rights Act 2015 or any other applicable UK consumer protection legislation to the extent such rights cannot lawfully be excluded.
If you are accessing Legacy as a client of Bit Trade Pty Ltd. in Australia: nothing in this Section S.12 excludes, restricts, or modifies any guarantee, condition, or warranty the exclusion of which would contravene the Competition and Consumer Act 2010 (Cth) or cause any part of this Section to be void.
13. Complaints
If you are accessing Legacy as a client of Payward Ltd. in the United Kingdom: you may submit a complaint in relation to Legacy to Kraken's complaints handling team. If you are not satisfied with Kraken's response, you may refer your complaint to the Financial Ombudsman Service at www.financial-ombudsman.org.uk .
14. Data Handling
Kraken's handling of your Recovery Material and personal data in connection with Legacy is governed by the applicable Kraken Privacy Notice for your jurisdiction. By using Legacy you consent to Kraken's processing of your Recovery Material in accordance with the applicable Privacy Notice.
15. Suspension and Termination
Kraken may suspend, restrict, or terminate your access to Legacy at any time and without notice, including pursuant to Section 15 of the Terms. Suspension or termination of your access to Legacy does not affect your other rights under the Terms.
Annex S-4
This Annex S-4 sets out the jurisdiction-specific terms applicable to users of Legacy whose Kraken account is held with Payward Trading Ltd. It supplements and must be read together with Annex S (Main Body). In the event of any conflict between this Annex S-4 and the Annex S Main Body, this Annex S-4 shall prevail.
For the avoidance of doubt, this Annex S-4 applies to all clients served through Payward Trading Ltd. under the Kraken Global Terms of Service. Where a jurisdiction-specific sub-annex is added for any such client's country, that sub-annex will prevail over this Annex S-4 to the extent of any inconsistency.
1. Offering Entity
Kraken Legacy is offered by Payward Trading Ltd. ("PTL").
2. Eligibility and Access
In addition to the eligibility requirements in Section 2 of the Annex S Main Body, users must be eligible to deposit and withdraw funds on the Kraken platform under PTL's terms. Legacy is not available in any jurisdiction where Kraken has expressly restricted access, where the offering of Legacy would require a local licence or registration that PTL does not hold, or where local law would prohibit the service.
3. Recovery Methods — Custodied Recovery Only
Recovery is currently available only to your Kraken custodial account held with PTL. All recovered Digital Assets will be transferred to Kraken-controlled deposit addresses associated with your verified Kraken account. Recovery to external self-custody wallets is not available at this time.
4. Fees
Any applicable fees will be clearly disclosed to you before you initiate Recovery and will require your affirmative acknowledgement before any fee is charged.
5. Succession and Estate Law — Important Notice
Possession of a seed phrase, private key, or hardware wallet backup does not, by itself, constitute lawful ownership of or lawful authority over the associated Digital Assets under the laws of any jurisdiction.
If you have received Recovery Material as part of a deceased person's estate, you should obtain independent legal advice under the laws of your jurisdiction before proceeding. The laws governing the administration of deceased estates, the vesting of title to personal property, and the authority of heirs or beneficiaries to transfer estate assets vary significantly across jurisdictions. PTL does not provide legal advice and does not adjudicate questions of ownership or estate entitlement.
By using Legacy, you represent that you are the lawful owner of, or have lawful authority to access, transfer, and recover, the Digital Assets associated with the Recovery Material you submit.
6. AML/CFT and Compliance
All Recovery transactions are subject to PTL's standard AML/CFT controls under applicable BVI law and regulations, including the Anti-Money Laundering and Terrorist Financing Code of Practice issued by the BVI FSC. Recovered Digital Assets flow through the same deposit screening processes as standard Kraken deposits. PTL may delay, block, or decline Recovery transactions where required by applicable law or PTL's compliance programme.
7. Data Handling
PTL's handling of your Recovery Material and personal data in connection with Legacy is governed by the applicable Kraken Privacy Notice . Recovery Material is processed solely for the purpose of providing Legacy and is permanently deleted upon completion or abandonment. PTL retains an action log for compliance purposes.
8. Local Law Compliance
You are responsible for ensuring that your use of Legacy complies with all applicable laws and regulations in your jurisdiction of residence, including any laws governing the inheritance, transfer, administration, or taxation of digital assets. PTL makes no representation that Legacy is lawful or appropriate for use in any particular jurisdiction beyond the scope of its BVI VASP registration. If you are uncertain whether Legacy is available or lawful in your jurisdiction, you should seek local legal advice before proceeding.
Annex T: US Card Services
The Cards offered by Kraken to US customers are issued by Lead Bank, a Missouri state-chartered bank and FDIC member. The card program is managed by Stripe, Inc. (“Stripe”). The accounts (“Krak accounts”) associated with the cards are provided by Payward Interactive, Inc. (“PWI”) which, trading as Kraken, is a FinCEN-registered Money Services Business (MSB) . Krak account balances held at PWI are not deposits of Lead Bank or any other insured depository institution, are not insured by the Federal Deposit Insurance Corporation (FDIC) or any other governmental agency, and may lose value.
1. Card Services Overview
a. Card Services Terms Overview. This Annex T (the “ Card Services Terms ”) governs the provision of Card services provided by PWI.
Before using our card services, you should ensure you have read and understood the risk warnings set out in Section ‎10 of the Terms. We have outlined some of the other substantial risks associated with using our services on our Legal Disclosures page , which is incorporated into these Card Services Terms by reference. You should carefully review these risks to determine if our services are suitable for you, considering your circumstances, knowledge, and financial resources.
b. Connection with the Terms. You acknowledge and agree that, except as expressly modified by the Card Services Terms, the terms and conditions in this Annex are supplemental to, and form part of the Kraken Terms of Service (the “ Terms ”). To the extent of any conflict, the terms of this Annex T will take precedence. We have used definitions in these Card Services Terms wherever we think it will be helpful, and capitalized terms used but not defined in these Card Services Terms shall have the meanings given to them in the Terms. Please also read our US Privacy Notice for information about how we collect, use, and share your personal information. Our Privacy Notice is incorporated into these Card Services Terms by reference.
c. The Card. We may offer you the ability to obtain a payment card (“ Card ”), which is issued to you by Lead Bank, with Stripe acting as program manager. The Card is governed by separate terms and conditions you enter into with Lead Bank (“ Lead Bank Terms ”) available here , which should be read alongside these Card Services Terms. The Card will be linked to your Krak account and will allow you to spend the balance in your Krak account to buy goods and services or withdraw cash in fiat currencies from merchants or ATMs that accept the Card. You agree that you will also use the Card in accordance with the Lead Bank Terms.
d. About Lead Bank and Stripe . Lead Bank is a Missouri state-chartered bank, with its principal offices at 1801 Main St, Kansas City, MO 64108. Lead Bank is the card issuer. Stripe is a Delaware corporation with principal offices at 354 Oyster Point Blvd, South San Francisco, CA 94080. Stripe acts as program manager and technology layer. Krak account balances held at PWI are not deposits of Lead Bank or any other insured depository institution and are not insured by the FDIC or any other governmental agency.
e. About the Card services . Upon a Card transaction, Stripe transmits a real-time authorization request to PWI, which performs eligibility, compliance, and balance checks. The amount that will be due from you in total to cover this will be the amount of the Card transaction, plus any accompanying fees (together the “ Total Transaction Price ”). By default, the funds that you will use to make a payment will be taken from, in turn:
i. First, the USD balance, then other Fiat Currency balances in your Krak account (in order from the largest balance to the smallest), then, as necessary;
ii. your available Digital Asset balance, first in stablecoins (in order from the largest balance to the smallest), then remaining Digital Assets (in order from the largest balance to the smallest), in which case PWI will sell the Digital Assets (a “ Digital Asset Transaction ”) and the USD proceeds (determined by the rates applicable at real-time authorization request — rates are available in the Krak app) will be used to settle the Total Transaction Price.
You can change the order in which your funds are spent to cover the Total Transaction Price at any time in the Krak app.
All Digital Asset Transactions are governed by the relevant sections of the Terms that set out your trading of Digital Assets, and the same considerations and restrictions apply. This means that, among other things, there are a number of situations in which a Digital Asset Transaction may be rejected, declined or fail, as explained in the Terms.
If your Fiat Currency balance(s) and Digital Assets in your Krak account cannot cover the Total Transaction Price, the transaction will generally be declined. If a negative balance occurs, it represents an amount you owe Lead Bank.
f. Your authorization . By taking out a Card, you expressly authorize us to use any Fiat Currency balance(s) and/or Digital Assets in your Krak account in order to settle Card transactions. You may give instructions at any time where supported in the Krak app if there are any Fiat Currency balances and/or Digital Assets that you do not want to be available to settle Card transactions. You can find details of any restrictions on the way in which we will use your Fiat Currency balances and/or Digital Assets in the Krak app. If, at the point of the transaction, there are insufficient funds to settle the Card transaction, then we reserve the right to decline the transaction as set out in more detail at section 5(a) of this Annex.
g. Availability of Digital Assets . Not all Digital Assets that you hold in your Krak account are available for Card transactions and the Digital Assets that are available may change from time to time. You will be notified when any additional Digital Assets become available to be used to settle Card transactions. From time to time, there may also be restrictions on the availability of the Digital Assets that you hold to be used to settle Card transactions (for example, if at the time of the Card transaction, there is a shortage of a particular Digital Asset in the market).
2. Card Transactions
a. Using the Card . You will need to authorize each Card transaction by following the procedures required for the type of payment you are making. You may need to take steps to authorize a transaction at the time you are paying, for example, by entering your Card details on a merchant’s online payment page, tapping your Card at the point of sale in-store or entering your PIN on the key pad. Depending on the circumstances, you may be required to undergo some further security checks at the time such as providing biometric data (e.g. a fingerprint).
b. Authorization. If the Card, card number and PIN or biometric data have been used, we will assume you have authorized the transaction. You must therefore tell us immediately if you do not recognize a transaction based on the information we provide to you. Please see section 2(f) below for information on how best to contact us. This presumption does not limit your right under applicable law, including your right to dispute unauthorized transactions under Regulation E.
c. Cancelling . You cannot cancel a Card transaction after it has been authorized. You can, however, cancel payments that you have authorized to be made on a future date, for example, if you have set up a recurring payment. To cancel a recurring payment relating to your Card, please first contact the relevant merchant. You may also contact us at +1 888 720 8670 or [email protected]
d. Timing . Below are the timings that apply to transactions under these terms:
i. Where you are settling the transaction with Digital Assets, any required Digital Asset Transaction will be executed by PWI at the time of your authorization/pre-authorization of the relevant Card transaction in line with the Terms; and
ii. Where you are settling the transaction with your Fiat Currency balance, we will initiate the transfer of the Card transaction monies to Stripe at the time of the authorization.
The maximum execution time of the Card transaction itself is not wholly within our or Stripe or Lead Bank’s control and is dependent on actions being taken by the merchant and the Visa network. Stripe and Lead Bank will comply with and adhere to the Visa network’s and our settlement timing requirements.
e. Chargebacks . We are not the provider of the goods or services purchased by you with your Card. All such disputes must be addressed directly with the merchant providing the relevant goods or services. Once you have authorized your Card to make a purchase, we cannot stop that Card transaction. However, where you have used your Card to buy goods or services, you may have a claim against the merchant if the goods or services are unsatisfactory, not supplied, supplied only in part or do not match the supplier’s description. You must notify us of any dispute and the chargeback will only be applied to your Krak account in USD if successfully secured from the merchant. Nothing in this section 2(e) limits or waives any rights you may have under applicable federal or state law, including your error resolution rights under section 2(f) below.
f. Unauthorized Card Transactions and Error Resolution.
i. Notifying us of errors. You must notify us promptly if you believe:
a Card transaction was unauthorized or executed incorrectly; or
an incorrect amount was debited from your Krak account in connection with a Card transaction.
You should notify us as soon as possible and in any event within 60 days of the date the relevant account statement was made available to you. Failure to notify us within this period may affect your ability to recover losses arising after the 60-day period.
ii. How to notify us. You may notify us of any suspected error or unauthorized transactions through the Krak app, by phone at +1 888 720 8670, or by other means through which you can reach us. See sections 12 and 13 of the Lead Bank Terms for more information regarding your liability for unauthorized transactions and for information regarding error resolution.
g. Errors . If we have made an error with respect to any Digital Asset Transaction to settle a Card transaction (for example, we’ve accidentally used a Digital Asset to settle a Card transaction that you have told us should not be available for Card transactions), we reserve the right, and you authorize us, to correct such error by revising the Digital Asset Transaction (including charging the correct price), cancelling the Digital Asset Transaction, or taking other reasonable action to resolve the error (including exchanging Fiat Currency or Digital Assets in your Krak account for alternative types of Fiat Currency or Digital Assets). Any fees or taxes deducted in connection with a correction will be limited to amounts you would have owed absent the error. Before taking corrective action under this section, we will notify you of the error and the steps we intend to take, except where delay would result in further loss to you or us. Our Terms set out further details of any liability potentially owed to you.
h. Limits . Our limits on your Card use are set out in the Krak app. Tap here (krakenpay://verification-and-limits) on your mobile device to learn more.
3. Charges and Currency Conversion
a. Settlement of transactions. When Lead Bank issues your Card, it will be denominated in USD. If you attempt to make a payment using your Card in a currency that is different to the currency in which the Card is denominated (even if you hold that currency in your Krak account), the payment will be automatically processed using a currency conversion rate to USD and you will be charged a 1% fee on the payment. Details of the fee as well as any applicable currency conversion will be displayed in the Krak app and the fee schedule.
b. Charges. When we sell Digital Assets in the context of a Card transaction, the price will include a spread (the difference between the market price and the price at which we execute the sale). The current spread is disclosed in the Krak app before you confirm each transaction and our fee schedule here . The spread may vary by Digital Asset and transaction size.
c. Exchange Rates. Currency conversions between the currency of a transaction and USD, as well as on Digital Asset Transactions, will be subject to the exchange rate at the time of the given Card transaction. You can find information on the exchange rates we use here ; the Krak app and our website will show details of the exchange rate applied. Any conversion is final at the point at which we authorize the transaction.
d. Changes to Charges and Exchange Rates . We may make changes to the applicable charges and exchange rates from time to time in accordance with section 6 (Our Fees) of the Terms and subject to the notice requirements in Section 4(f) (Amending these Card Services Terms) of this Annex.
e. Tax. You are responsible for determining and fulfilling any tax obligations that may arise in connection with your use of the Card and/or any Cashback you may receive, including but not limited to rewards related to Digital Assets such as bitcoin. You can find more information on taxes here .
4. Communications
a. General information. You may request a copy of these Card Services Terms at any time. By agreeing to these Card Services Terms, you also agree to the E-SIGN Disclosure and Consent, detailed at Section 19 of the Terms .
b. Communications from Lead Bank. As set out in the Lead Bank Terms, if Lead Bank needs to communicate with you in relation to the Card that they have issued you, such communications will be made through us (Kraken) using the methods set out in the Lead Bank Terms.
c. Information about Card transactions. We will provide you with a periodic statement of your Card transactions for each monthly cycle in which an electronic fund transfer has occurred, and at least quarterly otherwise. Each periodic statement will be accompanied by an error-resolution notice in accordance with Regulation E, informing you of your right to report errors and unauthorized transactions and the procedures for doing so. See Sections 12 and 13 of the Lead Bank Terms for more information.
d. Keeping your Card and its details safe. You must:
keep your Card, Card number, 3-digit security code and PIN safe;
not give your Card or its details (including the PIN) to anyone else;
not permit any other person to use your Card;
inform yourself about common scams, such as phishing; and
take all reasonable precautions to prevent the misuse of your Card and/or its details.
e. Telling us if your Card is lost, stolen or misused. If you believe that:
i. your Card has been lost, stolen or misused (whether for the making of unauthorized Card transactions or in any other way); and/or
ii. Card transactions in respect of your Card have either not been executed or have been defectively-executed; then
you must inform us immediately. The fastest way to reach us is through the Krak app, but you may also notify us by contacting our support team by phone, email, or any other available channel.
f. Amending these Card Services Terms. We may make changes to these Card Services Terms at any time in accordance with the preamble of the Terms and the notice requirements set out below. Where a change would result in increased fees, increased liability for you, fewer types of available electronic fund transfers, or stricter limitations on the frequency or dollar amount of transfers, we will provide you with written notice at least 21 days before the effective date of the change, as required by Regulation E.
5. Suspension, Cancellation and Termination
a. Refusal and Suspension. Transactions you attempt with your Card may be refused or declined and your access to the Card and all related services may be suspended with immediate effect where:
we are, in our reasonable opinion, required to do so by applicable law or any court or other authority to which we are subject in any jurisdiction;
we have concerns that a Card transaction is erroneous or about the security of your Card, your Krak account or we reasonably suspect our services are being used in a fraudulent or unauthorized manner;
we suspect money laundering, terrorist financing, fraud, or any other financial crime;
you have insufficient Fiat Currency and/or Digital Assets in your Krak account to cover the Total Transaction Price of a relevant Card transaction;
you have not provided us with information that we need to make the payment or we need to carry out further checks;
the payment exceeds any limits we tell you about when you try to make it;
a third party prevents us from making the payment (for example, the Visa network); and/or
we have suspended your Krak account.
Your Card may also be suspended if we reasonably :
suspect there has been unauthorized or fraudulent use of your Card;
have concerns about the security of your Card; or
believe that it is required in order to meet a legal or regulatory obligation that applies to us.
If a Card transaction is denied and/or your use of the Card and any related services is suspended in this way, we will (unless it would be unlawful for us to do so) provide you with notice of the actions and the reasons for the refusal or suspension together with the procedure for correcting any factual errors that led to the action. In the event that a Card transaction is declined and/or your use of the Card and any related service is suspended, the suspension will be lifted as soon as reasonably practicable once the reasons for declining and/or suspension no longer exist. You may contact us at any time to request a review of a suspension, and we will complete that review within 10 business days of your request. Lead Bank has additional rights to refuse Card transactions and suspend the use of the Card, as set out in the Lead Bank Terms.
b. Termination of these Card Services Terms by us. We may terminate this Annex T at any time in accordance with section 15 (Suspension; Termination; Discontinuance) of these Terms and without prior notice to you if:
we are, in our reasonable opinion, required to do so by applicable law or any court or other authority to which we are subject in any jurisdiction;
we reasonably suspect you of acting in breach of these Card Services Terms, this Agreement or the Lead Bank Terms;
we reasonably suspect our services are being used by you in a fraudulent or unauthorized manner; or
we reasonably suspect you of being engaged in money laundering, terrorist financing, fraud, or any other financial crime.
c. Termination of this Agreement and the Lead Bank Terms. These Card Services Terms will terminate automatically upon termination of these Card Services Terms and/or the Lead Bank Terms. In the event of such termination, we will notify you that any pending Card transactions have been suspended or declined. In any event, you will be required to pay any outstanding amounts owed to us.
6. Card Rewards
a. Eligible Cashback. Every time you make a Card transaction which is an eligible purchase, we will reward you with “ Cashback ” and give you back a percentage of the amount of the Card transaction. We explain what eligible purchases are in the Krak App and here . Cashback is a reward by us (and not Lead Bank, as issuer of your Card, nor any other party) for making a Card transaction, so it does not impact any offers merchants may also give you.
b. Ineligible transactions. The following transactions will not be eligible for Cashback:
taking out cash (including withdrawing cash from a cash machine)
transactions under 0.49 USD.
We reserve the right to change this list of ineligible transactions at any time.
c. How Cashback is calculated. The amount of any Cashback we will reward you with for any Card transaction is a percentage of the value of the Card transaction. The percentage that any Cashback is calculated on will be explained in the Krak App. We reserve the right to change the percentages of Cashback awarded at any time. We may set out multiple possible percentages in the Krak App and impose eligibility requirements that will determine which percentage is used to calculate Cashback on your Card transactions at that point in time. If you do not qualify for any possible percentages, none of your Card transactions will be an eligible purchase and you will not receive any Cashback for making a Card transaction.
d. Timing of Cashback. Any Cashback that arises from a Card transaction will be calculated in accordance with these Card Services Terms and made available to you shortly after settlement of that specific Card transaction in line with section 1(e) of this Annex.
e. Form of Cashback. Any Cashback due to you by us will be paid either in:
USD. Here any Cashback will be credited by PWI to your Krak account in the form of USD as part of the settlement of your Card transaction; or
Bitcoin. PWI will transfer the required value of bitcoin (calculated at the point of settlement of the transaction) to your Krak account. Cashback received in bitcoin may be treated as taxable income at the fair market value of the bitcoin at the time it is credited to your Krak account. See section 3(e) (Tax) for more information.
You can tell us that you wish to change the form your Cashback is paid to you in using the Krak App. The form in which Cashback is paid is subject to availability and we reserve the right to restrict or change the available and/or any default forms at any time.
f. Refunded Card Transactions. If a Card transaction is refunded then it immediately ceases to become an eligible purchase and you agree that:
you are no longer eligible to receive Cashback for that Card transaction; and
you will immediately return any Cashback that we have rewarded you with in relation to that Card transaction. Where you are required to return any Cashback, you authorize us to debit your Krak account in an amount equal to the value of the Cashback to be returned, and/or set off that amount against any amounts that we may otherwise owe you.
g. Disputed Card Transactions . You are also required to return Cashback that we have paid to you in error as well as for transactions where you acted fraudulently and/or engaged in activity designed to manipulate or artificially exploit the Card Rewards program (for example, making purchases solely for the purpose of earning Cashback with the intention of immediately returning the purchased goods or services). Where you are required to return any Cashback, you authorize us to take any steps required to do so in relation to your Krak account including but not limited to transferring any Fiat Currency or Digital Assets out of your Krak account, and/or setting off any amounts that we may otherwise owe you.
h. Missing Cashback : If you think you are missing Cashback, you need to tell us through the Krak App within 60 days of you making the relevant Card transaction. If, following our review, we decide you should have earned Cashback, we will pay it to you.
