i. Reward Disclaimer. All rewards are offered solely by us and not by Lead Bank (the issuer of your Card). Lead Bank is not responsible for, nor involved in, the provision or administration of any rewards associated with your Card.
j. Abuse of Card Rewards . If we reasonably suspect any fraud or misuse by you relating to Card Rewards, or if you breach the Card Services Terms, we may disqualify you from earning Cashback.
Changes to Card Rewards . We may make changes to the terms applying to Card Rewards, as well as withdraw it, at any time. You can check the Krak App at any time to find the latest Card Rewards available to you.
Annex U: Direct Deposit and Payroll Cash Advance Services
This Annex U (this “ Direct Deposit and Payroll Cash Advance Services Annex ”) incorporates by reference and supplements the Kraken.com Terms of Service (excluding all Annexes thereto, the “Terms”) as if the entirety of the Terms were set forth in this Direct Deposit and Payroll Cash Advance Services Annex. Capitalized terms used but not defined in this Direct Deposit and Payroll Cash Advance Services Annex have the meanings given to them elsewhere in the Terms. In the event of a conflict between the Terms and this Direct Deposit and Payroll Cash Advance Services Annex, the terms of this Direct Deposit and Payroll Cash Advance Services Annex will control solely to the extent of the conflict and solely with respect to the subject matter of this Direct Deposit and Payroll Cash Advance Services Annex.
1. Eligibility and Enrollment
If you are eligible, you may arrange to have all or part of your paycheck deposited into your Krak account (the “Direct Deposit Services”). Direct Deposit Services are currently available to eligible users in the United States only.
a. Self-Enrollment. You may enroll by providing your employer or payroll provider with the account and routing number Kraken assigns to your account. The account and routing number are provided solely to facilitate electronic receipt of payroll payments. They do not represent a bank account in your name. Your first direct deposit may take one to three payroll cycles to take effect, as determined by your employer or payroll provider.
b. Switch-Assisted Enrollment. Where available, you may also enroll using our switch-assisted service, which uses Underdog Technologies, Inc. dba Pinwheel (“Pinwheel”) to submit a deposit switching instruction to your payroll provider on your behalf. Depending on your payroll provider's capabilities, this instruction may be submitted either (i) automatically, by providing information that identifies you to Pinwheel so it can locate and match your payroll record without requiring you to log in to your payroll provider, or (ii) through a secure authenticated connection you initiate.
2. Payment Processing
Direct deposits are processed via the ACH network. Kraken utilizes third-party payment processors and regulated financial institutions (“Payment Partners”) to receive and process your direct deposit. By using Direct Deposit Services, you authorize Kraken and its Payment Partners to act as the receiving institution for your ACH credit entries under the NACHA Operating Rules and applicable law.
3. Pinwheel
If you use switch-assisted enrollment, the following additional terms apply.
a. Pinwheel. Pinwheel is our third-party payroll connectivity provider. By using switch-assisted enrollment, you authorize Kraken and Pinwheel to: (i) provide and use information that identifies you, together with known payroll provider information, in order to locate and match your payroll record and submit a deposit switching instruction on your behalf; (ii) where you initiate an authenticated connection, access your payroll provider account for the purpose of submitting a deposit switching instruction; and (iii) retrieve your payroll account information as described in Section 6. Pinwheel may provide its services through affiliated entities. Pinwheel’s own Terms of Service and Privacy Policy apply to the services Pinwheel provides in connection with your Krak account and are available at https://www.pinwheelapi.com/about/terms and https://www.pinwheelapi.com/about/terms#privacyPolicy .
b. No Guarantee of Switching Success. Kraken does not guarantee that a switching instruction submitted through Pinwheel will be accepted or completed by your payroll provider by any particular payroll cycle. Some payroll providers may delay or reject switching instructions. You remain responsible for confirming the switch has taken effect with your payroll provider. Kraken is not responsible for any paycheck directed to a prior account after a switching instruction has been submitted.
4. Fees
Direct Deposit Services, including direct deposit switching, are provided to you at no charge. Fees for the Payroll Cash Advance Service are shown in the Krak app (Section 9(c)). Fees may apply to other Krak products and services, which will be disclosed to you separately before you enroll.
5. Salary Match
Eligible users who set up direct deposit with Krak may receive a Salary Match credit to their Krak account, calculated as a percentage of qualifying payroll deposits over the period set out in the Krak app. The applicable percentage, the period, any minimum or maximum amounts, any eligibility requirements, and any conditions on the use of Salary Match credits will be set out in the Krak app. We may offer multiple percentage tiers, and the percentage applied to your account will be determined by your eligibility at the time of qualifying deposits. We reserve the right to change the Salary Match percentage or percentages, period, minimum or maximum amounts, eligibility requirements, and any other terms applicable to Salary Match at any time. Salary Match credits are not cash and their use may be subject to additional terms displayed in the Krak app or on the Krak website.
6. Payroll Data
If you use switch-assisted enrollment, your authorization under Section 3 also covers Kraken’s ongoing access to your payroll account information via Pinwheel. We use this information to verify and confirm deposit switching instructions, match incoming payroll deposits to your account, and detect fraud. We will not use your payroll data for eligibility determinations for any financial product or for any other purpose without first notifying you and obtaining your separate consent. For Payroll Cash Advance, that separate consent is given under Section 9(a). For a description of the specific categories of payroll information we collect and how we handle it, please see our Privacy Notice.
7. Funds Availability
The availability of your direct deposit funds depends on the timing of your employer’s or payroll provider’s funding processes. Funds are typically available on or around your scheduled pay date. Availability is not guaranteed and may vary by payer, payroll provider, and payroll schedule. Deposits may be delayed for holidays or other processing delays outside our control.
8. Reversals and Returns
If a credited direct deposit is reversed or returned, Kraken will reverse the credit by debiting your Krak account in the amount of the reversal and will recover such amount by applying funds available in your Krak account, including any subsequent direct deposit.
9. Payroll Cash Advance
If you are eligible and enroll in our payroll cash advance service (“Payroll Cash Advance Service”), the additional terms in this Section 9 apply. In the event of a conflict between this Section 9 and any other Section of this Direct Deposit and Payroll Cash Advance Services Annex or any other provision of the Kraken.com Terms of Service (including any other Annex), this Section 9 will control to the extent of the conflict and solely with respect to the Payroll Cash Advance Service. The Payroll Cash Advance Service is a direct-to-consumer earned wage access service that lets you request an advance of a portion of your pay (an “Advance”). It is currently available to eligible users in the United States only, in the states where we make it available. It is provided by Payward Rewards LLC or another of our affiliates that we designate (the “Advance Provider”), and references to “we” and “us” in this Section 9 include the Advance Provider.
a. Eligibility and Enrollment. To use the Payroll Cash Advance Service, you must have received recurring salary deposits into your Krak account that we have identified as your pay. We identify salary deposits using our own analysis of deposits into your Krak account, which may include each deposit’s sender and transfer details, your deposit history and, where you have connected a payroll account, the payroll information we retrieve from it (see Sections 3 and 6). If you did not use switch-assisted enrollment, you must connect your payroll account through Pinwheel, and Sections 3 and 6 apply to that connection. You may not be eligible until we have identified enough deposits. Cash, ATM and mobile deposits, transfers from other accounts, and peer-to-peer or mobile wallet payments do not count as salary, and it may take several consecutive deposits before we identify a deposit as recurring salary. Advances are not available if your most recent salary deposit is overdue. Your Krak account must be in good standing, and you must not have had an Advance written off under Section 9(e) on any Krak account. You enroll by accepting these terms in the Krak app. Your rights under Section 9(g) and the fees that apply to the Payroll Cash Advance Service are shown in the Krak app before you enroll and before each Advance. By enrolling, you consent to our use of your payroll data, including your deposit history and details, and any payroll information we retrieve under Section 6, to determine whether you are eligible for the Payroll Cash Advance Service and the amount available to you. This is the separate consent referred to in Section 6, and we will not use your payroll data for any other eligibility determination without first notifying you and obtaining your separate consent.
b. How Advances Work. You may request an Advance once you are eligible. Advances are subject to limits. The amount you may request in each Advance, and the total amount of Advances available to you in any rolling 30-day period, are shown in the Krak app, and we will not make more available than those amounts. We determine these amounts based on your representations about your employer and pay and our reasonable determination of your earned but unpaid pay, which we derive from the salary deposits we have identified in your Krak account. When you request an Advance, you represent that the employer and pay information you provide is accurate and complete, that the pay you are accessing is due to you, and that no one else has paid you any part of it. Your Advances are grouped into cycles. A cycle starts when you make your first Advance and ends when a salary deposit settles in your Krak account that covers all of your open Advances under Section 9(d), and salary deposits that settle on the same day are treated together. After a cycle ends, you may start a new cycle if you remain eligible. You may make more than one Advance in a cycle, up to the limits shown in the Krak app. You cannot request an Advance during the 48 hours before your next salary deposit is expected, as shown in the Krak app. We identify salary deposits using our own analysis, which may be wrong or may change, and we may adjust or stop making Advances available to you if it does. Advances are delivered to your Krak account.
c. Delivery Options and Fees. When you request an Advance, you may choose either (i) standard delivery, at no cost to you, which we will start no later than one (1) business day after you request the Advance, with funds generally available within 24 to 48 hours after your request, or (ii) instant delivery, for a fee (the “Instant Delivery Fee”). The Krak app explains how to choose the no-cost option. The Instant Delivery Fee and any other fees that apply to the Payroll Cash Advance Service are shown in the Krak app before you confirm each Advance.
d. Recovery. Once a salary deposit that we identify settles in your Krak account after an Advance, you authorize us to recover the amount of each open Advance, together with any Instant Delivery Fee, from your available balance in your Krak account. When that deposit covers all of your open Advances, your cycle ends. We recover automatically, and we may do so when the deposit is credited or at any time afterward. We recover whole Advances, oldest first, each with its Instant Delivery Fee and no other fee. If the deposit does not cover all of your open Advances, any remaining Advance stays open and is recovered from your next salary deposit, and the end date of your cycle does not change. A deposit that is reversed or returned no longer counts as salary and does not count toward recovery. If we credit your Krak account wholly or partially in error in connection with an Advance, you authorize us to correct the error by making a corrective debit or credit. If a deposit used to recover an Advance is reversed or returned, the Advance is treated as unpaid, and Section 8 applies only to the reversed amount.
e. Unrecovered Advances; No Collection or Credit Reporting. A cycle may last no longer than 34 days. If your open Advances have not been recovered in full by the end of the cycle, we will write off the unpaid amount and you will no longer be eligible for the Payroll Cash Advance Service. Unpaid amounts do not roll over, accrue interest or become available to you as a new Advance. You have no obligation to repay an Advance, and we will have no legal or contractual claim or remedy against you based on a failure to repay it, other than recovery from your Krak balance under Section 9(d). We will not (i) charge or collect interest, a late fee, a deferral fee or any other charge or penalty for non-payment, (ii) report your payment or non-payment to any consumer reporting agency, or (iii) try to compel payment by unsolicited outbound telephone calls, by suing you, by using a third party to collect from you, or by selling the amount to a collector or debt buyer. Nothing in this Section 9(e) limits our right to pursue amounts obtained through fraud or other unlawful means.
f. Salary Match. Salary Match credits under Section 5 are separate promotional credits. They are not part of the amount available as an Advance, they do not increase that amount, and you may not request an Advance against them.
g. Cancellation and Your Rights. You may cancel the Payroll Cash Advance Service as described in Section 10. In connection with the Payroll Cash Advance Service, you have the right to (i) choose the no-cost delivery option every time you request an Advance, (ii) see all fees before you request an Advance, (iii) cancel at any time without a fee, and (iv) receive notice of material changes under Section 9(h) before they apply to you. These rights are in addition to any rights you have under applicable law.
h. Changes to the Payroll Cash Advance. We will notify you of any material change to the terms or fees of the Payroll Cash Advance Service, including a change in the Advance Provider, in the Krak app and by email before the change takes effect for you, and where required by law we will obtain your consent. If you do not agree to a change, you may cancel the Payroll Cash Advance Service under Section 10 before it takes effect. This Section 9(h) applies notwithstanding Sections 5, 12 and 13 to the extent they would allow a change to the Payroll Cash Advance Service without notice.
10. Modifications and Cancellation
You may change your direct deposit amount or cancel Direct Deposit Services at any time by: (i) updating your deposit instructions directly with your employer or payroll provider; or (ii) if you used switch-assisted enrollment, disabling Direct Deposit Services in the Krak app. Changes and cancellations of direct deposit take effect only when processed by your payroll provider and typically require one to two payroll cycles. Kraken does not control your payroll provider’s processing timelines and is not responsible for delays or missed deposits resulting from changes made with your payroll provider. Disabling Direct Deposit Services in the Krak app will revoke Pinwheel’s ongoing access to your payroll provider account. Disabling Direct Deposit Services ends your eligibility for new Advances. You may cancel the Payroll Cash Advance Service at any time in the Krak app, at no charge. Neither cancelling the Payroll Cash Advance Service nor disabling Direct Deposit Services changes Sections 9(d) or 9(e) for an outstanding Advance.
11. Personal Information
You agree that we may share personal information you have provided to us, in accordance with our Privacy Notice, with Payment Partners and Pinwheel to process deposits, make and recover Advances, verify and match identity, manage risk, and comply with applicable law. Kraken’s Privacy Notice is available at kraken.com/legal/privacy.
12. Termination
We reserve the right to suspend or terminate your access to Direct Deposit Services or Payroll Cash Advance Service at any time, including if you violate these terms, your Krak account is suspended or terminated, or we are required to do so by applicable law. Suspension or termination does not change how an outstanding Advance is recovered under Section 9(d) or written off under Section 9(e), and Sections 9(d) and 9(e) continue to apply after you stop using the Payroll Cash Advance Service or your account or the Terms end.
13. Limits and Restrictions
We may, at our sole discretion, set limits or restrictions on direct deposit transactions. We reserve the right to accept, reject, or limit deposits via Direct Deposit Services in our sole discretion. Limits on Advances are set out under Section 9(b).
Annex V: AI Interaction Service
This Annex V (this “ AI Interaction Annex ”) incorporates by reference and supplements the Kraken.com Terms of Service (excluding all Annexes thereto, the “Terms”) as if the entirety of the Terms were set forth in this AI Interaction Annex. The service governed by this AI Interaction Annex (the “ AI Interaction Service ”) is available only if you live in the United States of America or Australia and is provided by Payward Wallet LLC (“ PWLLC ”, “ we ”, “ us ”, “ our ”). In this AI Interaction Annex, “we”, “us” and “our” refer only to PWLLC and do not include any other Payward affiliate. In the event of a conflict between this AI Interaction Annex and the Terms, this AI Interaction Annex controls solely to the extent of the conflict and solely with respect to the AI Interaction Service.
RISK NOTICE.
The AI Interaction Service uses artificial intelligence. What it tells you may be wrong, incomplete or out of date, and no one can guarantee any result. You are responsible for your own decisions. PWLLC is not registered or licensed as an investment adviser, broker-dealer or commodity trading advisor with the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission, the U.S. Commodity Futures Trading Commission or any state regulator.
1. The AI Interaction Service
a. What it is. The AI Interaction Service lets you interact, in the Kraken interface, with a conversational artificial intelligence tool (the “ AI Tool ”). When you submit questions and instructions and continue the conversation (“ Prompts ”), the AI Tool responds with market commentary, analysis and feedback, including on your holdings and trading activity and on assets, products and features available through Kraken (“ Outputs ”). The AI Tool is configured by us and runs on a large language model provided by OpenAI and its affiliates (the “ Model Provider ”).
i) Account Information. To produce Outputs, the AI Tool draws on information in your Kraken account, such as your balances, holdings and trading history (“ Account Information ”).
ii) Eligibility. You may use the AI Interaction Service only if you live in the jurisdictions noted in the introduction to this Annex, hold a Kraken account in good standing, and accept this AI Interaction Annex. We may make the AI Interaction Service available to some users and not others, and may change eligibility at any time.
iii) Identification as artificial intelligence. The AI Tool is labelled in the Kraken interface as artificial intelligence and identifies PWLLC as its provider. You are not communicating with a human being, and Outputs are not reviewed by a human before you see them.
2. Using Outputs
a. Your decisions. Outputs are one input to your own decision-making. You decide whether to act on an Output, and you are solely responsible for every decision you make and every order you place or approve, including any made after reading an Output.
b. Outputs may be wrong. The AI Tool uses a large language model that predicts likely text. Outputs may be wrong, incomplete, outdated, inconsistent between sessions, or presented with more confidence than is warranted. The AI Tool may invent facts, figures, sources or events, and market data it states may not be current. Outputs may not reflect all of your circumstances.
c. No guarantee of results. We make no representation about the accuracy, completeness, timeliness or reliability of any Output, or that any Output will be consistent with prior Outputs or with information elsewhere on our Services and Platforms. Outputs may refer to past performance, forecasts or scenarios. None of these is a guarantee or a reliable indicator of future results.
d. Independent professionals. You are responsible for deciding whether to consult an independent legal, tax or financial professional.
e. Orders and availability. The AI Tool does not manage your account. No order or transfer is placed on your account unless you place or approve it yourself in the Kraken interface. Any such order is placed with the entity that provides your trading services under the Terms and is governed by the Terms and the Annexes that apply to those services. PWLLC is not a party to those orders.
3. Fees and Conflicts of Interest.
We do not charge you a fee for the AI Interaction Service. Other Payward affiliates earn fees when you buy, sell, stake or otherwise transact on Kraken. Outputs that discuss transactions may lead you to make transactions that generate those fees. This gives Payward a commercial interest in your trading activity on Kraken.
4. Use of the AI Tool.
In addition to the restrictions in the Terms, you must not attempt to circumvent the safeguards or limits of the AI Tool, or use the AI Tool or Outputs to train, develop or improve any other artificial intelligence model.
5. Liability and Indemnity.
The AI Interaction Service and all Outputs are provided “as is” and “as available”. The Disclaimer of Warranties, Limitation of Liability and Indemnification sections of the Terms apply to the AI Interaction Service and to PWLLC, the Model Provider and their respective affiliates, as if references in those sections to “we” or “Kraken” included PWLLC.
6. Availability and Changes.
We may modify, limit, suspend or end the AI Interaction Service, or your access to it, at any time and without notice, including to meet legal or regulatory requirements, to address risk, or because you breached the Terms or this AI Interaction Annex. Ending the AI Interaction Service does not affect your Kraken account or any order you have placed.
Annex W: Krak Spend Card Services
This Annex W (this “Card Annex”) incorporates by reference and supplements the Kraken.com Terms of Service (excluding all Annexes, the “Terms”). Capitalized terms not defined in this Card Annex have the meanings given in the Terms. If this Card Annex conflicts with the Terms, this Card Annex controls, but only to the extent of the conflict and only for its subject matter.
This Card Annex governs the provision of the Krak Spend Card Services (as defined below) in connection with the Krak Spend Card (the “Card”) by Payward Trading Ltd (“PTL”, “we”, “us”), and your rights and obligations in relation to those services. It supplements, and should be read together with, the Terms and the Underlying Agreement (as defined below), and is an integral part of the Terms.
The Cards offered under this Card Annex are credit cards issued to you by Reap Borderless MX, S.A. de C.V., a company incorporated in Mexico (“Reap MX”), under the Underlying Agreement (as defined below), to which you, Reap MX and PTL are parties. The Krak Spend Card Services are provided by PTL, which holds your Account and the Eligible Assets in it. PTL is not the issuer of the Card and is not a lender to you. Neither the Card nor your Account is a bank deposit or electronic money (electronic payment funds).
1. Definitions and Construction
Unless the context otherwise requires, capitalized terms and expressions used but not defined in this Card Annex have the meanings given to them in the Terms or the Underlying Agreement. In this Card Annex:
“Available Assets” means the aggregate value of all Eligible Assets held by you in your Account that are not Frozen Assets.
“Krak Spend Card Services” means the services provided by PTL under this Card Annex in connection with the Krak Spend Card, comprising the holding, safekeeping, administration, freezing (lock-up), and release or application of Settlement Assets in your Account strictly for the purpose of settling Card transactions in accordance with valid instructions, the Terms, and this Card Annex.
“Settlement Order” means the order in which asset types are reserved as Reserved Assets and applied under the Settlement Instruction, as you select in the Krak app and change from time to time.
“Eligible Assets” means the forms of Fiat Currency and Digital Assets that we designate as eligible for the Krak Spend Card Services from time to time, that are held in your Account.
“PTL Settlement Account” means the account held in PTL’s own name, separate from customer accounts, designated by us to receive Frozen Assets applied in accordance with Section 5(c) (Successful Completion).
“Settlement Assets” means the Eligible Assets in your Account that are available to be frozen, or that are frozen, applied or released, pursuant to this Card Annex.
“Spending Power” means at any time, the lower of (a) your available Credit Limit and any other limits applied by PTL or Reap MX from time to time, and (b) the value of your Available Assets.
“Standing Authority” means the Settlement Instruction you give us under the Underlying Agreement, to reserve Eligible Assets on each authorization and apply them on Clearing, as supplemented by Section 5(e) (Your Authorization).
“Underlying Agreement” means the Krak Spend Card Cardholder Terms and Conditions among you, Reap MX and PTL governing the Card, the Credit Line and the settlement of card transactions, as in force from time to time. The Underlying Agreement is separate from this Card Annex and is not part of the Terms.
2. Krak Spend Card Services Overview
a. Overview of the Card. Reap MX may offer you the Card, which is a credit card issued to you by Reap MX. The Card and the credit made available through it are provided by Reap MX under the Underlying Agreement, and you agree to use the Card in accordance with it. The Krak Spend Card Services are provided by PTL separately from, and are not part of, the Underlying Agreement.
b. Roles of Reap MX and PTL. The roles of Reap MX and PTL are set out in the Underlying Agreement. Reap MX does not hold or administer your Account or any of your assets, and you have no claim against Reap MX for the return of any asset held in your Account.
c. Account and Eligible Assets. The Krak Spend Card Services operate on the Eligible Assets held in your Account under the Terms. Eligible Assets that are frozen, applied or returned under this Card Annex remain subject to the Terms (including Section 2 (Custody and Title of Digital Assets) of the Terms) except as expressly provided in this Card Annex. Notwithstanding the restriction in Section 2 of the Terms on the use of Fiat Currency held in your Account, Fiat Currency that is an Eligible Asset may also be reserved and applied as Reserved Assets and as consideration for the Debt Assumption under the Underlying Agreement.
d. Appointment and Role of PTL. You appoint PTL to provide the Krak Spend Card Services, subject to the Terms and this Card Annex. Subject to this Card Annex, we may assume that all instructions given are in compliance with the Underlying Agreement and shall not be held liable for carrying out the instructions.
e. Availability and Eligibility. The Krak Spend Card Services are available only to clients of PTL who have been approved for a Card by Reap MX, and only in jurisdictions where we and Reap MX have made the Card available.
3. Instructions to PTL
a. In General. You herewith irrevocably instruct PTL, and PTL herewith agrees, to only accept and execute any instruction in relation to the Settlement Assets which is explicitly provided for in this Card Annex or the Underlying Agreement. Your instructions include the Standing Authority, your Settlement Order under Section 5(a), and your withdrawals of Available Assets under Section 6(b).
b. Reap MX’s Instructions. Reap MX’s instructions include its notifications of authorizations, settlements, refunds and reversals under Sections 5 and 7, and its instructions in respect of the Secured Assets under the Underlying Agreement.
c. Inconsistency. If there is any inconsistency between the Terms and this Card Annex in connection with the form of instruction and release of the Settlement Assets, this Card Annex prevails. As between you and PTL, the Underlying Agreement prevails on the Card, the Credit Line, the Settlement Instruction, the Debt Assumption, the pledge and the Reserved Issuer Functions, and this Card Annex prevails on your Account, fees charged by PTL, shortfalls, refunds and provisional credits.
4. Assumption on Clearing
a. Assumption on Clearing. On Clearing of each card transaction, PTL, or another Payward group entity approved by Reap MX (the “Payer”), assumes your obligation to pay the corresponding Outstanding Amount to Reap MX, in consideration of the assets applied under Section 5(c), and you are released from that obligation, as set out in the Underlying Agreement. You have no rights against, and may not direct, any Affiliate that pays Reap MX on the Payer’s behalf.
b. Shortfalls. Any difference between an Outstanding Amount assumed under Section 4(a) and the value of the Frozen Assets applied is owed by you to PTL and is dealt with under Section 9. If the assumption under Section 4(a) does not take effect on Clearing, the transfer under Section 5(c) does not take effect, the Frozen Assets remain frozen, and you remain liable to Reap MX as provided in the Underlying Agreement.
5. Settlement of Card Transactions
Notwithstanding anything to the contrary, the processing and settlement of card transactions shall automatically proceed strictly in accordance with the following mechanics:
a. Authorization and Settlement Order. PTL confirms to Reap MX in real time whether you have sufficient Spending Power to be frozen, and Reap MX decides whether to authorize the card transaction, as a Reserved Issuer Function. For the purpose of settlement, you may set your Settlement Order in the Krak app and change it at any time for future card transactions. PTL will use your Eligible Assets in your Settlement Order and, if the first asset type is insufficient, will use the next asset type to cover the remainder. For the avoidance of doubt, PTL will not use, freeze or deduct Eligible Assets that are not in your Settlement Order to settle a card transaction, except as provided in Section 9 (Shortfalls and Automatic Deduction). A Settlement Order determines only which Eligible Assets are reserved and applied on Clearing. It does not entitle you to use Eligible Assets to pay a merchant. Each card transaction is paid by Reap MX under the Credit Line.
b. Asset Freezing (Hold). If you have sufficient Spending Power, PTL will freeze the required amount of Eligible Assets, applying your Settlement Order (the “Frozen Assets”, being the Reserved Assets under the Underlying Agreement) in your Account. The amount of Digital Assets frozen will be determined based on a real-time reference spot rate provided by PTL. This reference spot rate is utilized solely for the purpose of calculating the required amount of Digital Assets to hold and does not constitute a currency conversion executed on your behalf. The Frozen Assets shall be locked without undergoing immediate conversion. Frozen Assets remain in your Account and may not be withdrawn, traded or otherwise dealt with while frozen.
c. Successful Completion. If the card transaction is successfully executed with the merchant and cleared through the Visa network, PTL shall, upon notification from Reap MX of the settled amount, unfreeze the Frozen Assets and transfer the underlying assets to the PTL Settlement Account pursuant to your Standing Authority, as consideration for the assumption under Section 4(a) in respect of that card transaction. You acknowledge that title to the assets so transferred passes to PTL on transfer (which takes effect together with the assumption under Section 4(a)), that the transfer may be a taxable disposal, and that you are responsible for any resulting taxes in accordance with Section 6 of the Terms. Any subsequent conversion of such Digital Assets into Fiat Currency is performed by PTL strictly for its own account and not on your behalf.
d. Transaction Failure. If the card transaction is declined, fails, is cancelled or reversed, or otherwise does not occur or clears for a lower amount, PTL will unfreeze the Frozen Assets (or the excess) on notification from Reap MX or when the authorization lapses, thereby restoring them to your Available Assets. If an authorization is increased or a card transaction is cleared in parts, PTL will adjust the Frozen Assets accordingly.
e. Your Authorization. The Standing Authority is your continuing authorization and instruction to us to freeze Settlement Assets and to apply Frozen Assets in accordance with this Section 5. It continues until the Card and the Underlying Agreement have terminated, all card transactions have been settled and all amounts owed under Section 9 have been paid.
f. Exchange Fee and Currency Conversion. Where Digital Assets are transferred to PTL under Section 5(c), PTL charges the exchange fee included in the Card Services Fees (as defined in Section 10(b)) (the “Exchange Fee”) in place of a conversion fee. The Exchange Fee is PTL’s fee for exchanging the Digital Assets and is not a charge made by Reap MX for the Card. The reference spot rate and any currency conversion rate applicable to a card transaction are shown in the Krak app. Card transactions are settled in US dollars. Where a card transaction is in another currency, PTL applies a foreign exchange fee as shown in the Krak app. The foreign exchange fee is a Card Services Fee and is not a charge made by Reap MX.
6. Release and Transfer of Assets
a. Release by PTL. PTL shall disburse, transfer or release the Frozen Assets strictly in accordance with valid instructions pursuant to this Card Annex. PTL may refuse an instruction from you if you are in breach of this Card Annex.
b. Your Withdrawals. You may at any time withdraw or transfer all or part of the Available Assets from your Account in accordance with Section 3 of the Terms, provided that such assets are not currently Frozen Assets as detailed in Section 5(b). Your withdrawal requests shall be processed in real-time. If Settlement Assets have been frozen under Section 5(b) before your withdrawal request is executed, the freeze takes priority, and PTL shall release only the remaining Available Assets in accordance with your withdrawal request. You shall indemnify PTL against any costs, loss, liability or expense whatsoever which may be suffered or incurred by PTL directly or indirectly in connection with carrying out instructions pursuant to your withdrawal request.
7. Refunds and Provisional Credits
a. Refunds. Refunds, reversals and successful chargebacks are returned to your Account in Fiat Currency only, regardless of which Eligible Asset was reserved, frozen or applied, and you waive any right to the return of the specific Digital Asset. PTL credits refunds within the period set out in the Underlying Agreement.
b. Provisional Credits. Where required by applicable law, or where we elect to do so, we may credit your Account with a provisional credit in Fiat Currency in respect of a disputed card transaction pending the outcome of the dispute. The amount, timing and conditions of any provisional credit are set out in the Krak app and may vary by country. A provisional credit is conditional: if the dispute is not resolved in your favor, or you receive a refund or other recovery for the same transaction, you authorize us to reverse it by deducting the amount from your Account, and Section 9 applies to any shortfall. We have no obligation to provide a provisional credit except as required by applicable law.
8. Operational Thresholds and Transaction Limits
The processing and settlement of card transactions, including the calculation, freezing, and transfer of any Digital Asset pursuant to your Standing Authority, are subject to minimum and maximum operational thresholds (including, without limitation, minimum transfer quantities, network dust limits, and platform system limits). PTL and Reap MX reserve the right, in accordance with the Underlying Agreement and applicable law, to decline or refuse to process any card transaction if the required amount of Digital Asset to be transferred falls below the applicable minimum operational threshold or exceeds the maximum operational limit. You acknowledge and agree that such operational thresholds are determined dynamically and are applied systematically in the background without prior notice, real-time display, or per-transaction acknowledgement.
9. Shortfalls and Automatic Deduction
If the value of the Frozen Assets applied on Clearing is less than the Outstanding Amount assumed under Section 4(a) (including because the cleared amount differs from the authorized amount or the value of the Frozen Assets has changed), or you incur any other shortfall in connection with card transactions, chargebacks or fees (a “Shortfall”), the Shortfall is owed by you to PTL and not to Reap MX. You irrevocably authorize PTL to deduct the equivalent Fiat Currency or Digital Asset value from other Eligible Assets in your Account, including assets credited later. You shall not set off any amount you owe under this Card Annex, the Terms or the Underlying Agreement against any amount owed to you by PTL or Reap MX.
10. Statements, Fees and Cashback
a. Statements. We will provide you with periodic statements through the Krak app showing your card transactions, the Frozen Assets, and the assets applied under Section 5(c).
b. Fees. You shall pay fees for the Krak Spend Card Services as set out in the Krak App or otherwise notified to you (the “Card Services Fees”). PTL reserves the right to deduct unpaid Card Services Fees directly from the Eligible Assets in your Account prior to executing any withdrawal request under Section 6(b).
c. Changes to Fees. We may make changes to the Card Services Fees from time to time in accordance with Section 6 (Fees; Taxes) of the Terms.
d. Cashback. Any cashback or other reward on card transactions is offered solely by PTL and not by Reap MX or the Visa network. Eligibility, rates, the form in which cashback is paid and any excluded transactions (such as cash withdrawals) are set out in the Krak app, and we may change or withdraw cashback at any time. Cashback is credited to your Account after the relevant card transaction settles. If a card transaction is refunded, reversed or successfully disputed, or if cashback was paid in error or in connection with fraud or abuse of the rewards program, that cashback is no longer due and you authorize us to deduct its value from your Account, and Section 9 applies to any shortfall. You are responsible for any taxes on cashback, and PTL may report or withhold as required by applicable law.
11. Suspension and Termination
a. Refusal and Suspension. We may withhold our confirmation of a card transaction and suspend your access to the Krak Spend Card Services in the circumstances described in Section 15 (Suspension; Termination; Discontinuance) of the Terms. Reap MX may refuse card transactions and suspend the Card and the Credit Line under the Underlying Agreement. If a card transaction is refused or your use of the Krak Spend Card Services is suspended, we will (unless unlawful) notify you of the action and the reasons for it, and will lift the suspension as soon as reasonably practicable once those reasons no longer exist.
b. Termination by PTL. We may terminate this Card Annex, including by ceasing to provide the Krak Spend Card Services, in accordance with Section 15 (Suspension; Termination; Discontinuance) of the Terms. If we cease to act, the Credit Line terminates as provided in the Underlying Agreement, and card transactions authorized before termination will still be settled under Section 6.
c. Termination of the Underlying Agreement. This Card Annex terminates automatically upon termination of the Underlying Agreement or the Card. The Standing Authority and the assumption under Section 4(a) and your obligations under Section 9 continue in respect of card transactions authorized before termination, and Frozen Assets will be applied or released in accordance with Sections 5 and 6.
12. Limitation of Liability
PTL’s liability to you under this Card Annex is limited as set out in Section 12 (Limitations on Liability) of the Terms, and its liability under the Underlying Agreement is limited as set out in that agreement. Nothing in this Card Annex limits or excludes any liability that cannot be limited or excluded under applicable law.
